## Sheet: `Wage Type Catalogue`

| Mercans Code | Wage Type Name in English | Wage Type Name in Local Language | Wage Type Explanation | Category | Formula | Tax Code | Calculation Type | Prorating Method | Occurrence | Ignore If 0 | Enforce Setting | In Period Reconciliation | Subject to Tax | Table Tax | Special Tax | Subject to Social Insurance | Subject to Pension | YTD Load (Yes/ No) | YTD Remarks |

| 21100 | Basic salary reduction - unpaid absence | Vermindering basisloon - onbezoldigde afwezigheid |  | Deduction | Value of the unpaid day(s), computed with the same proration method used for entry and exit - market standard is the working-day method: monthly salary x working days not covered / total working days in the month. Reduces the NSSO wage and the taxable base of the period; the day is declared in the DmfA with the applicable absence code, which also drives whether it is an equivalent day for holiday accrual. | 250 |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes |  |  |
| 21102 | Basic salary reduction - retroactive correction (current year) | Vermindering basisloon - retroactieve correctie (lopend jaar) |  | Deduction | Retroactive correction within the same calendar year. Added to the current period's ordinary remuneration and taxed once at the ordinary Annex III scale (26.75% / 42.80% / 48.15% / 53.50%); no separate annualisation. Corrective DmfA for the quarter to which the wage relates, at 13.07% employee and the employer rates of that quarter. | 250 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes |  |  |
| 21103 | Recovery of salary advance (Wage Protection Act art. 23) | Terugvordering voorschot op loon (Loonbeschermingswet art. 23) |  | Deduction | Recovery of an advance already paid. Taken from NET pay after all statutory levies. Aggregate of the Article 23 deductions (advances, fines, damage, security deposits) may not exceed one fifth of the net wage due at each payment; any unrecovered balance is carried forward to the next period and never taken out of the protected floor. Belgian correction rule for a taxable and contributory item: a correction relating to the CURRENT calendar year is added to the period's ordinary remuneration and taxed once at the ordinary Annex III scale, while a correction relating to a PREVIOUS year is arrears taxed at the exceptional-remuneration percentage keyed on the monthly gross x 13.92. Social security follows the period of work, so the amount is declared through a corrective DmfA for the original quarter at the 13.07% employee rate and the employer rates then in force; this re-opens the special social security contribution band and the social work bonus for that quarter. | NA |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA |  |  |
| 21108 | Salary reduction - incapacity beyond the guaranteed salary period | Vermindering loon - arbeidsongeschiktheid na gewaarborgd loon |  | Deduction | Applied from the day the employer-funded guaranteed salary ends (white-collar: after 30 calendar days; blue-collar: after the day-1-to-30 scale). Income replacement is taken over by the health insurance fund at 60% of the capped gross during the first year of incapacity. Days are valued with the working-day proration method and declared in the DmfA with the incapacity absence code; they remain equivalent days for holiday accrual. | 250 |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes |  |  |
| 21109 | Salary reduction - unpaid leave, thematic leave and time credit | Vermindering loon - onbetaald verlof, thematisch verlof en tijdskrediet |  | Deduction | Value of the unpaid day(s), computed with the same proration method used for entry and exit - market standard is the working-day method: monthly salary x working days not covered / total working days in the month. Reduces the NSSO wage and the taxable base of the period; the day is declared in the DmfA with the applicable absence code, which also drives whether it is an equivalent day for holiday accrual. Ordinary unpaid leave is NOT an equivalent day and reduces the following year's holiday entitlement and holiday pay; thematic leave and time credit with a motive ARE assimilated within their limits. The contract is suspended, not terminated - master data, benefits and statutory declarations continue. | 250 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes |  |  |
| 21110 | Compensatory rest recovery - overtime at 50% supplement | Recuperatie inhaalrust - overuren met 50% toeslag |  | Deduction | Belgium compensates overtime primarily by paid rest (inhaalrust), only the supplement being paid in cash. This line reverses the ordinary hourly pay already included in the monthly salary for the hours taken as compensatory rest, so that the 38-hour weekly average is respected over the reference period without paying the hour twice. Reduces the NSSO wage and the taxable base. | 250 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes |  |  |
| 21111 | Compensatory rest recovery - overtime at 100% supplement (Sunday / public holiday) | Recuperatie inhaalrust - overuren met 100% toeslag (zondag / feestdag) |  | Deduction | Belgium compensates overtime primarily by paid rest (inhaalrust), only the supplement being paid in cash. This line reverses the ordinary hourly pay already included in the monthly salary for the hours taken as compensatory rest, so that the 38-hour weekly average is respected over the reference period without paying the hour twice. Reduces the NSSO wage and the taxable base. | 250 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes |  |  |
| 21112 | Overtime correction - current calendar year | Correctie overuren - lopend kalenderjaar |  | Deduction | Retroactive correction within the same calendar year. Added to the current period's ordinary remuneration and taxed once at the ordinary Annex III scale (26.75% / 42.80% / 48.15% / 53.50%); no separate annualisation. Corrective DmfA for the quarter to which the wage relates, at 13.07% employee and the employer rates of that quarter. | 250 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes |  |  |
| 21113 | Recovery of overtime paid in advance | Terugvordering vooruitbetaalde overuren |  | Deduction | Recovery of overtime settled before the hours were confirmed by the time record. Taken from net within the one-fifth aggregate cap of Article 23 of the Wage Protection Act of 12 April 1965; corrective DmfA for the quarter in which the hours were originally declared. | NA |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA |  |  |
| 21114 | Reversal - overtime supplement above the 180-hour tax relief ceiling | Terugname - overwerktoeslag boven de grens van 180 uren |  | Deduction | The employee reduction of 66.81% (57.75% for a 20% supplement) and the employer exemption of 41.25% (32.19%) apply to a maximum of 180 hours per employee per calendar year, made permanent from 2026. This line reverses the relief already granted on hours that turn out to exceed the ceiling; the excess hours are then taxed normally at the ordinary Annex III scale. The hour counter is reported in 281.10 box 305 (zone 2.142). | 233 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes |  |  |
| 21115 | Reversal - voluntary overtime above the 360-hour annual quota | Terugname - vrijwillige overuren boven het jaarquotum van 360 uren |  | Deduction | From 1 April 2026 the voluntary overtime quota per employer is 360 hours per year (450 in the hotel and catering sector). Hours above the quota lose the voluntary-overtime regime and revert to ordinary overtime: full 13.07% employee and ordinary employer contributions and ordinary withholding. Reported in 281.10 box 317 (zone 2.110). | 317 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes |  |  |
| 21116 | Reversal - voluntary net overtime above the 240-hour exempt tranche | Terugname - netto vrijwillige overuren boven de vrijgestelde schijf van 240 uren |  | Deduction | Of the 360-hour voluntary quota applicable from 1 April 2026, the first 240 hours may be paid without an overtime supplement and are exempt from ordinary social security contributions and from wage withholding tax. This line reverses the exemption on hours found to fall outside that tranche, restoring the 13.07% employee contribution and ordinary withholding. | 317 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes |  |  |
| 21117 | Recovery - overtime paid in error, prior quarter | Terugvordering - ten onrechte betaalde overuren, vorig kwartaal |  | Deduction | The employee repays the NET amount received; the employer recovers the social security and the withholding tax through a corrective DmfA for the original quarter and a corrected monthly withholding tax return. Recovery from net is capped at one fifth of the net wage per payment under Article 23 of the Wage Protection Act. | NA |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA |  |  |
| 21118 | Compensatory rest recovery - night work | Recuperatie inhaalrust - nachtarbeid |  | Deduction | Belgium compensates overtime primarily by paid rest (inhaalrust), only the supplement being paid in cash. This line reverses the ordinary hourly pay already included in the monthly salary for the hours taken as compensatory rest, so that the 38-hour weekly average is respected over the reference period without paying the hour twice. Reduces the NSSO wage and the taxable base. | 250 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes |  |  |
| 21119 | Compensatory rest recovery - shift work | Recuperatie inhaalrust - ploegenarbeid |  | Deduction | Belgium compensates overtime primarily by paid rest (inhaalrust), only the supplement being paid in cash. This line reverses the ordinary hourly pay already included in the monthly salary for the hours taken as compensatory rest, so that the 38-hour weekly average is respected over the reference period without paying the hour twice. Reduces the NSSO wage and the taxable base. | 250 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes |  |  |

This line reverses the ordinary hourly pay already included in the monthly salary for the hours taken as compensatory rest, so that the 38-hour weekly average is respected over the reference period without paying the hour twice. | 21121 | Reversal - horeca overtime relief, employer without registered cash register | Terugname - vrijstelling overuren horeca, werkgever zonder geregistreerd kassasysteem |  | Deduction | Reverses the exemption declared in 281.10 box 335 (zone 2.061) with the hour count in box 336, where the hours are found to fall outside the hotel and catering overtime scheme. The hours revert to the ordinary regime: 13.07% employee contribution, ordinary employer contributions and ordinary Annex III withholding. | 335 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes |  |  |
| 21122 | Reversal - horeca overtime relief, employer with registered cash register | Terugname - vrijstelling overuren horeca, werkgever met geregistreerd kassasysteem |  | Deduction | Reverses the exemption declared in 281.10 box 395 (zone 2.143) with the hour count in box 396. The higher horeca ceiling is conditional on the registered cash register (geregistreerd kassasysteem); where the condition fails the hours revert to the ordinary overtime regime. | 395 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes |  |  |
| 21123 | Reversal - construction overtime relief above the electronic-attendance ceiling | Terugname - vrijstelling overuren bouw boven de grens met registratiesysteem |  | Deduction | The construction sector benefits from a higher overtime relief ceiling only where an electronic attendance registration system is in use; the hours are declared in 281.10 zone 2.176 within the total of box 305. This line reverses relief granted on hours above that ceiling. | 305 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes |  |  |
| 21124 | Claw-back - employee overtime withholding reduction 66.81% | Terugname - vermindering bedrijfsvoorheffing overuren 66,81% |  | Deduction | Reverses the employee reduction of 66.81% of the base on which a 50% or 100% overtime supplement was calculated (Income Tax Code 1992 art. 154bis). The relief is computed on the BASE of the supplement, not on the supplement itself, and is reported in 281.10 box 233 (zone 2.067) with the hour count in zone 2.095. | 233 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | NA | NA |  |  |
| 21125 | Claw-back - employee overtime withholding reduction 57.75% (20% supplement) | Terugname - vermindering bedrijfsvoorheffing overuren 57,75% (toeslag 20%) |  | Deduction | Reverses the employee reduction of 57.75% applicable where the statutory supplement is 20%. Reported in 281.10 box 234 (zone 2.068) with the hour count in zone 2.097. | 234 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | NA | NA |  |  |
| 21127 | Claw-back - employer overtime withholding exemption 41.25% / 32.19% | Terugname - vrijstelling doorstorting bedrijfsvoorheffing overuren 41,25% / 32,19% |  | Deduction | Reverses the employer exemption from remitting part of the wage withholding tax on the base of the overtime supplement - 41.25% where the supplement is 50% or 100%, 32.19% where it is 20% (ITC 92 art. 275/1). This is an employer cash item settled in the monthly withholding tax return through Finprof; it does not change the employee's net. | NA |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA |  |  |
| 21140 | Recovery - meal voucher employee part correction | Terugvordering - correctie werknemersaandeel maaltijdcheques |  | Deduction | Corrects the employee part withheld from net. The employee part must be at least EUR 1.09 per voucher and the face value may not exceed EUR 10.00 from 1 January 2026, with one voucher per day actually worked and electronic issue. Breaching any single condition requalifies the WHOLE voucher as ordinary wage retroactively, with contributions due on the entire face value. | NA |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA |  |  |
| 21141 | Recovery - eco vouchers issued in error | Terugvordering - ten onrechte toegekende ecocheques |  | Deduction | Eco vouchers are excluded from the NSSO wage concept and from taxable income only within the sectoral ceiling and their own conditions; the scheme is being phased out. Vouchers issued outside the conditions are requalified as ordinary wage in full and this line recovers the value from net within the Article 23 one-fifth cap. | NA |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA |  |  |
| 21142 | Requalification - bicycle allowance above EUR 0.37 per km | Herkwalificatie - fietsvergoeding boven EUR 0,37 per km |  | Deduction | The bicycle allowance is exempt from tax and from social security up to EUR 0.37 per kilometre and EUR 3,700 per year for 2026; the CBA no. 164 obligation itself is EUR 0.30 per km for up to 40 km per day. The excess over EUR 0.37/km or over the annual ceiling is ordinary wage: 13.07% employee contribution, employer contributions and ordinary withholding. | 254 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes |  |  |
| 21143 | Requalification - homeworking allowance above EUR 160.99 per month | Herkwalificatie - thuiswerkvergoeding boven EUR 160,99 per maand |  | Deduction | The flat office (homeworking) allowance is exempt from tax and contributions up to EUR 160.99 per month from 1 March 2026 (EUR 157.83 before that date). The excess is ordinary wage and attracts the 13.07% employee contribution, employer contributions and ordinary withholding. | 250 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes |  |  |
| 21144 | Recovery - commuting allowance overpaid | Terugvordering - te veel betaalde vergoeding woon-werkverkeer |  | Deduction | Recovery of a commuting reimbursement paid for a period no longer covered (change of address, season ticket returned, homeworking). Exempt commuting reimbursement is reported in the 281.10 box 254 total; the recovery reduces that total for the year. | 254 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | No | No | No | No | No |  |  |
| 21145 | Recovery - end-of-year premium paid in advance | Terugvordering - vooruitbetaalde eindejaarspremie |  | Deduction | Many sectoral CBAs make the end-of-year premium conditional on service at the payment date and forfeit it on resignation before that date. Recovery is from net within the Article 23 one-fifth cap; the employee repays the net received and the employer recovers the 13.07% contribution and the withholding through a corrective DmfA and a corrected 281.10. | NA |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA |  |  |
| 21146 | Requalification - CBA no. 90 bonus above the EUR 4,255 social ceiling | Herkwalificatie - CAO 90-bonus boven het sociaal plafond van EUR 4.255 |  | Deduction | Within the social ceiling of EUR 4,255 for 2026 the non-recurring result-tied bonus carries a 33% special employer contribution and a 13.07% employee solidarity contribution in place of ordinary contributions. The excess above the ceiling becomes ordinary wage subject to full employee and employer contributions. Note the deliberate gap with the tax ceiling of EUR 3,701 - the amount between EUR 3,701 and EUR 4,255 is socially exempt but taxable. | 242 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes | No | Yes | Yes | Yes |  |  |
| 21147 | Requalification - flexi-job wage above the EUR 18,440 exemption | Herkwalificatie - flexi-jobloon boven de vrijstelling van EUR 18.440 |  | Deduction | The flexi-wage is exempt from wage withholding tax up to EUR 18,440 for income year 2026 (281.10 box 262, zone 2.198, for a non-pensioner). The excess is taxable. The employer's 28% special contribution continues to apply to the flexi-wage and the flexi holiday pay. | 262 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | No | No |  |  |
| 21148 | Requalification - student wage beyond the 650-hour annual quota | Herkwalificatie - studentenloon boven het jaarquotum van 650 uren |  | Deduction | Within the first 650 hours of student work per calendar year the solidarity contribution of 2.71% employee and 5.42% employer replaces ordinary contributions and no wage withholding tax is due. From the 651st hour ordinary rules apply: 13.07% employee, approximately 25% employer and ordinary Annex III withholding. The employer must check the remaining quota through the student counter before engaging. | 250 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes |  |  |
| 21149 | Requalification - unjustified flat-rate expense allowance | Herkwalificatie - niet-verantwoorde forfaitaire onkostenvergoeding |  | Deduction | Reimbursement of expenses proper to the employer is excluded from the NSSO wage concept and from taxable income only where it is genuine and justified, either on actual cost or on a flat rate accepted by the NSSO and the tax administration. An allowance that cannot be justified is requalified as ordinary wage in full. 281.10 distinguishes flat allowances meeting serious norms (zone 2.200) from those that do not (zone 2.078) and cost-based reimbursements (2.201). | 250 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes |  |  |
| 21150 | Shift premium reduction - unpaid absence | Vermindering ploegenpremie - onbezoldigde afwezigheid |  | Deduction | Fixed monthly premiums are reduced in the same proportion as the basic salary unless the sectoral CBA provides otherwise; premiums expressed per day or per worked hour are simply not counted for the absent days. Same proration method as the basic salary. | 250 |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes |  |  |
| 21151 | Night premium reduction - unpaid absence | Vermindering nachtpremie - onbezoldigde afwezigheid |  | Deduction | Night premiums are paid per hour actually worked at night, so an unpaid absence simply removes the hours. Where the sectoral CBA expresses the premium as a fixed monthly amount, it is reduced with the same proration method as the basic salary. | 250 |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes |  |  |
| 21152 | Standby / on-call premium reduction - unpaid absence | Vermindering wachtdienstpremie - onbezoldigde afwezigheid |  | Deduction | Standby premiums follow the standby roster actually performed. Reduced for periods of suspension of the contract; declared in the DmfA with the applicable absence code. | 250 |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes |  |  |
| 21190 | Salary reduction - long-term incapacity (health insurance fund takes over) | Vermindering loon - langdurige arbeidsongeschiktheid (ziekenfonds neemt over) |  | Deduction | After the employer-funded guaranteed salary period the employee's income is paid by the health insurance fund under the National Institute for Health and Disability Insurance at 60% of the capped gross during the first year of incapacity. Payroll stops the salary, produces the DmfA absence code and the electronic scenario declaration (ASR) that triggers the benefit, and pays any sectoral supplement. From 1 January 2026 the relapse period is 8 weeks (previously 14 days) and no guaranteed salary is due for an incapacity arising during progressive work resumption. | 250 |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes |  |  |
| 21210 | Overtime hours recovered - 38-hour average reference period settlement | Recuperatie overuren - afrekening referteperiode 38-urenweek |  | Deduction | Sectoral CBAs may organise the 38-hour weekly average over a reference period of up to one year, in which case hours above the weekly schedule inside that period are NOT overtime and carry no supplement. This line settles the credit hours taken as rest inside the reference period. Labour Act of 16 March 1971, art. 20bis. | 250 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes |  |  |
| 21211 | Overtime hours recovered - flexible working time schedule (CBA) | Recuperatie overuren - flexibele arbeidsregeling (CAO) |  | Deduction | Recovery of hours worked above the fixed schedule under a CBA-organised flexible working time arrangement, where the average of 38 hours per week must be respected over the reference period. No overtime supplement is due on these hours. | 250 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes |  |  |
| 21212 | Overtime hours recovered - 120 additional recovery hours | Recuperatie overuren - 120 bijkomende recuperatie-uren |  | Deduction | Under the voluntary overtime scheme in force until 31 March 2026 the annual quota of 100 hours could be extended to 220 by counting the 120 additional recovery hours. This line records the recovery of those hours as rest. From 1 April 2026 the quota per employer is 360 hours. | 250 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes |  |  |
| 21213 | Overtime hours recovered - end of reference period balancing | Recuperatie overuren - afsluiting referteperiode |  | Deduction | End-of-reference-period balancing: hours still in credit when the reference period closes must either be taken as rest or paid with the statutory supplement. This line records the part settled as rest; the part paid in cash is carried on the corresponding overtime earning. Belgian correction rule for a taxable and contributory item: a correction relating to the CURRENT calendar year is added to the period's ordinary remuneration and taxed once at the ordinary Annex III scale, while a correction relating to a PREVIOUS year is arrears taxed at the exceptional-remuneration percentage keyed on the monthly gross x 13.92. Social security follows the period of work, so the amount is declared through a corrective DmfA for the original quarter at the 13.07% employee rate and the employer rates then in force; this re-opens the special social security contribution band and the social work bonus for that quarter. | 250 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes |  |  |
| 21400 | Employee personal contribution for employer-provided housing | Eigen bijdrage werknemer voor terbeschikkinggestelde woning |  | Deduction | The employee's own contribution is deducted from the taxable benefit in kind for free accommodation but can never make the benefit negative. The benefit itself is valued at a multiple of the indexed cadastral income under Article 18 of the Royal Decree implementing ITC 92 and reported on 281.10 with benefit-nature code B. | 250 |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes |  |  |
| 21520 | Employee contribution / recovery - commuting allowance | Eigen bijdrage / terugvordering - vergoeding woon-werkverkeer |  | Deduction | Employee share of a season ticket or of organised collective transport, or recovery of the employer intervention where the entitlement lapses. The exempt employer intervention is reported in the 281.10 box 254 total (zones 2.086 public transport, 2.087 organised transport, 2.088 other means). | 254 |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | No | No | No | No | No |  |  |
| 21610 | Hospitalisation insurance - employee premium for family members | Hospitalisatieverzekering - werknemersbijdrage voor gezinsleden |  | Deduction | The employer-paid premium covering the employee is exempt from contributions and tax where the policy meets the conditions. Cover extended to family members and paid by the employee is withheld from NET pay and gives no withholding reduction. Deducted after all statutory levies. | NA |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA |  |  |
| 21611 | Guaranteed income (disability) insurance - employee personal contribution | Gewaarborgd inkomen (invaliditeit) - persoonlijke bijdrage werknemer |  | Deduction | Personal contribution to a guaranteed income / disability top-up cover. Where the cover is an accessory of the second-pillar pension plan it is treated with that plan and the contribution generates the 30% withholding reduction; where it is a stand-alone policy it is a pure net deduction with no relief. | NA |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA |  |  |
| 21640 | Personal contribution to the supplementary pension plan (2nd pillar) | Persoonlijke bijdrage aanvullend pensioen (2e pijler) |  | Deduction | BELGIUM-SPECIFIC DIVERGENCE FROM THE GLOBAL LABEL: this is NOT a pre-tax deduction in Belgium. The personal contribution is withheld from NET pay and relieved through a reduction of the wage withholding tax equal to 30% of the contribution withheld, applied under Annex III at the withholding step (see BE-007). Only the statutory 13.07% employee contribution is a genuine pre-tax deduction. Reported on 281.10 in box 285 (zone 2.081), ordinary contributions and premiums. The plan itself is constrained by the 80% rule, not by a payroll cap. | 285 |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA |  |  |

Permitted: exchanging a FUTURE discretionary bonus or increase for a benefit. Prohibited: reducing acquired salary below the sectoral minimum or the GMMI, and sacrificing pay already due. Any arrangement converting contributory wage into a non-contributory benefit without a genuine change in entitlement is requalified as wage by the NSSO. | NA |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA |  |  |

Pure net deduction. | NA |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA |  |  |
| 21890 | Employee contribution towards the stock option / warrant cost | Eigen bijdrage werknemer in de kostprijs van aandelenopties / warrants |  | Deduction | Any amount paid by the employee for the option itself reduces the taxable benefit valued at grant under the Law of 26 March 1999. The benefit is 18% of the value of the underlying shares (9% where the plan meets the conditions), increased by 1 percentage point (0.5 pp under the 9% rate) per year or part-year of the exercise period beyond 5 years from the offer. Taxation is at grant, not at exercise. Belgian correction rule for a taxable and contributory item: a correction relating to the CURRENT calendar year is added to the period's ordinary remuneration and taxed once at the ordinary Annex III scale, while a correction relating to a PREVIOUS year is arrears taxed at the exceptional-remuneration percentage keyed on the monthly gross x 13.92. Social security follows the period of work, so the amount is declared through a corrective DmfA for the original quarter at the 13.07% employee rate and the employer rates then in force; this re-opens the special social security contribution band and the social work bonus for that quarter. | NA |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA |  |  |
| 21900 | Employee global social security contribution - 13.07% | Persoonlijke RSZ-bijdrage werknemer - 13,07% |  | Deduction | THE single global employee contribution (globaal beheer). Base x 13.07%, NO CEILING. For blue-collar workers the base is the gross increased by 8% (gross x 108%), because their holiday pay is funded through the holiday fund. The 13.07% covers pensions 7.50%, medical care 3.55%, sickness and disability benefits 1.15% and unemployment 0.87% - there is no separate pension deduction in Belgium. Reduced by the social work bonus for low wages, which since 1 April 2024 has TWO components computed separately and added (P = R_B + R_A, wage type BE-002): from 1 July 2026 the white-collar maxima are EUR 171.99 for component B and EUR 127.54 for component A, EUR 299.53 combined (blue-collar EUR 185.75 and EUR 137.74, EUR 323.49 combined). Each component pays its maximum ONLY at or below its own full-amount limit and is degressive above it - do not grant a full maximum anywhere below the upper limit. Never below zero. Deducted from the gross BEFORE the taxable base is formed (ITC 92 art. 52, 3 degrees). | NA |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA |  |  |

It is replaced by a special employer contribution of 8.86% on the premium (see BE-021) plus the 4.4% insurance premium tax (BE-022). Blue-collar: the whole base is the gross at 108%, giving approximately 30.57%. Employer contributions are NOT due on the double holiday pay. The basic and wage moderation contributions are not due on the part of the quarterly wage above EUR 86,700 from 1 January 2026 (see BE-033). | NA |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA |  |  |
| 23672 | Employer CO2 solidarity contribution - company car | CO2-solidariteitsbijdrage werkgever - bedrijfswagen |  | Employer Contribution | Employer-only NSSO levy, due monthly per vehicle made available for private use, independent of the employee's income and of the taxable benefit, and REPLACING ordinary contributions on the car benefit. Monthly contribution = [ (CO2 in g/km x 9.00) - F ] / 12 x 1.6291, where F = 768 petrol, 600 diesel, 990 LPG, and 1.6291 is the 2026 index coefficient (health index September 2025 = 185.85 / September 2004 = 114.08). Minimum EUR 33.93 per month for vehicles ordered before 1 July 2023 and EUR 42.34 from 1 July 2023. Default CO2 where unknown: 182 g/km petrol, 165 g/km diesel. Electric and hydrogen vehicles always owe the minimum. Multiplier for combustion vehicles ordered from 1 July 2023: 4.00 for 2026, fixed by Article 34, 1e of the Law of 25 November 2021 organising the fiscal and social greening of mobility (Belgian Official Gazette 3 December 2021, numac 2021033910), which completes Article 38, par. 3quater, 3e of the Law of 29 June 1981 with the schedule 2.25 from 1 July 2023, 2.75 from 1 January 2025, 4.00 from 1 January 2026 and 5.50 from 1 January 2027, and is reproduced by NSSO administrative instructions 2026/2 (special contributions, company vehicles). The multiplier applies to the CALCULATED contribution only, never to the minimum, and not at all to vehicles ordered before 1 July 2023. Corrects an earlier 2.75 figure for 2026, which is the 2025 value. Declared per vehicle in the quarterly DmfA, prorated by day where availability starts or ends mid-month. | NA |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA |  |  |

Added to the ordinary monthly remuneration and NOT treated as an exceptional payment. | 62180 | Notice indemnity (opzeggingsvergoeding / verbrekingsvergoeding) | Opzeggingsvergoeding / verbrekingsvergoeding |  | Earning | Indemnity = current remuneration x length of the statutory notice period. 'Current remuneration' includes ALL benefits acquired under the contract: the taxable value of the company car, the employer share of meal vouchers, variable pay averaged over the preceding 12 months, the pro rata end-of-year premium and holiday pay, and group and hospitalisation insurance premiums. Notice table for contracts started from 1 January 2014 rises to 65 weeks; for contracts whose performance started from 1 June 2026 the notice is CAPPED AT 52 WEEKS from 17 years of seniority; from 1 August 2026 the first six months of seniority carry a uniform notice of 1 week. Resignation notice is capped at 13 weeks. Where the notice or indemnity is at least 30 weeks, outplacement is due and 4 weeks of the indemnity may be deducted. THERE IS NO TAX-FREE PORTION: fully sociable (13.07% employee and ordinary employer contributions) and fully taxable. Withheld at the Annex III nr. 58 arrears-and-severance percentage, to which nr. 62 routes notice indemnities - NOT at the nr. 53 exceptional-payment percentage, which excludes notice indemnities in terms (andere exceptionele vergoedingen en toelagen dan opzeggingsvergoedingen). Under nr. 62 the percentage is keyed on the remuneration that served as the basis for determining the indemnity, not on the monthly gross x 13.92. The nr. 58 scale has 18 bands: 0.00% up to EUR 11,860.00; 2.68% to 14,235.00; 6.57% to 15,810.00; 10.77% to 18,980.00; 13.55% to 20,565.00; 16.55% to 22,935.00; 19.17% to 26,885.00; 24.92% to 34,790.00; 29.93% to 42,695.00; 31.30% to 55,355.00; 36.90% to 62,465.00; 38.96% to 71,160.00; 40.93% to 83,020.00; 42.92% to 99,630.00; 44.99% to 124,925.00; 46.47% to 143,905.00; 47.48% to 169,205.00; 48.00% above. It tops out at 48.00%, below the nr. 53 top rate of 53.50%, and is built to approximate the art. 171 average rate; finally taxed separately at the average rate of the last previous year of normal professional activity (ITC 92 art. 171, 5 degrees, a). | 62500 | Company car benefit in kind | Voordeel van alle aard bedrijfswagen |  | Earning | ANNUAL benefit = catalogue value x CO2 percentage x 6/7 x age coefficient, with an annual FLOOR of EUR 1,690 for 2026 (EUR 140.83 per month); charged at one twelfth per month. CO2 percentage = 5.5% + 0.1% x (vehicle CO2 - reference CO2), floored at 4% and capped at 18%. Reference CO2 for 2026: 70 g/km petrol, LPG and natural gas; 58 g/km diesel. Age coefficient: 100% in the first 12 months from first registration, then reduced by 6 percentage points per elapsed year, floored at 70%. The employee's own contribution is deducted but can never make the benefit negative. CRITICAL BELGIAN ASYMMETRY: the car benefit is EXCLUDED from the NSSO wage concept - no ordinary employee or employer contributions - and is REPLACED by the employer CO2 solidarity contribution (23672). It is nevertheless FULLY TAXABLE. Prorated by day of AVAILABILITY, not by attendance, so it is unaffected by unpaid absence. Reported on 281.10 in zone 2.076 with benefit-nature code F. | 250 |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | No | No |  |  |

Where it is paid BY THE FUND it is not employer wage; where the employer pays it and recovers it from the fund it is carried here. Ordinary wage where it is consideration flowing from the employment relationship. Belgian correction rule for a taxable and contributory item: a correction relating to the CURRENT calendar year is added to the period's ordinary remuneration and taxed once at the ordinary Annex III scale, while a correction relating to a PREVIOUS year is arrears taxed at the exceptional-remuneration percentage keyed on the monthly gross x 13.92. Social security follows the period of work, so the amount is declared through a corrective DmfA for the original quarter at the 13.07% employee rate and the employer rates then in force; this re-opens the special social security contribution band and the social work bonus for that quarter. | 250 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes | No | Yes | Yes | Yes |  |  |
| 62740 | Study allowance for the employee children - taxable | Studietoelage voor kinderen van de werknemer - belastbaar |  | Earning | Outside the inbound taxpayer regime, an employer contribution to the school or study costs of an employee's children is NOT an expense proper to the employer: it is a benefit obtained by reason of the employment, taxable under ITC 92 art. 31 and within the NSSO wage concept. | 250 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes |  |  |
| 62741 | Arrears - study allowance for the employee children | Achterstallen - studietoelage voor kinderen van de werknemer |  | Earning | Arrears of a taxable study allowance. Where the arrears relate to a previous calendar year they take the exceptional-payment percentage keyed on the monthly gross x 13.92. Belgian correction rule for a taxable and contributory item: a correction relating to the CURRENT calendar year is added to the period's ordinary remuneration and taxed once at the ordinary Annex III scale, while a correction relating to a PREVIOUS year is arrears taxed at the exceptional-remuneration percentage keyed on the monthly gross x 13.92. Social security follows the period of work, so the amount is declared through a corrective DmfA for the original quarter at the 13.07% employee rate and the employer rates then in force; this re-opens the special social security contribution band and the social work bonus for that quarter. | 252 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes | No | Yes | Yes | Yes |  |  |

Belgian correction rule for an exempt item: the ceiling tested is that of the PERIOD TO WHICH the amount relates, not that of the payment date, and a change of ceiling applies from its effective date without recomputing earlier periods. The item stays outside the NSSO wage concept and outside the taxable base only while every condition of its own regime is met; where a condition fails, Belgium requalifies the amount as ordinary wage IN FULL - 13.07% employee contribution and ordinary employer contributions - through a corrective DmfA for the original quarter and a corrected form 281.10. | NA |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | No | No | No | No | No |  |  |

Remains excluded from the base where the professional link and the supporting documents are in order. Belgian correction rule for an exempt item: the ceiling tested is that of the PERIOD TO WHICH the amount relates, not that of the payment date, and a change of ceiling applies from its effective date without recomputing earlier periods. The item stays outside the NSSO wage concept and outside the taxable base only while every condition of its own regime is met; where a condition fails, Belgium requalifies the amount as ordinary wage IN FULL - 13.07% employee contribution and ordinary employer contributions - through a corrective DmfA for the original quarter and a corrected form 281.10. | NA |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | No | No | No | No | No |  |  |

Belgian correction rule for a taxable and contributory item: a correction relating to the CURRENT calendar year is added to the period's ordinary remuneration and taxed once at the ordinary Annex III scale, while a correction relating to a PREVIOUS year is arrears taxed at the exceptional-remuneration percentage keyed on the monthly gross x 13.92. Social security follows the period of work, so the amount is declared through a corrective DmfA for the original quarter at the 13.07% employee rate and the employer rates then in force; this re-opens the special social security contribution band and the social work bonus for that quarter. | 250 |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes |  |  |

Belgian correction rule for a taxable and contributory item: a correction relating to the CURRENT calendar year is added to the period's ordinary remuneration and taxed once at the ordinary Annex III scale, while a correction relating to a PREVIOUS year is arrears taxed at the exceptional-remuneration percentage keyed on the monthly gross x 13.92. | 62890 | Stock options accepted within 60 days - taxable benefit at grant | Aandelenopties aanvaard binnen 60 dagen - belastbaar voordeel bij toekenning |  | Earning | Law of 26 March 1999. THE DEFINING FEATURE IS THAT TAXATION OCCURS AT GRANT, NOT AT EXERCISE. The employee must accept the offer IN WRITING WITHIN 60 DAYS. Flat valuation: 18% of the value of the underlying shares, reduced to 9% where the plan meets the conditions of the Law (the option cannot be exercised before the end of the third calendar year after grant nor after the tenth year, the exercise price is fixed at grant, and the option is not transferable), INCREASED BY 1 PERCENTAGE POINT (0.5 percentage point under the 9% rate) PER YEAR OR PART-YEAR of the exercise period beyond 5 years from the offer. For listed shares the underlying value is the average price over the 30 days preceding the offer, or the closing price on the day before the offer. NO social security contributions, provided the employer does not guarantee the exercise gain and the option relates to shares of the employer or of a group company. The gain realised on exercise or sale is in principle not taxed again as professional income. Processed in the payroll of the month of acceptance; reported on 281.10 in zone 2.082 for options granted in the year, with the percentages used in zone 2.106 and a foreign-grantor flag in zone 2.039. | 250 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | No | No |  |  |

Fully sociable and taxed at the ordinary Annex III scale. TWO CHANGES TOOK EFFECT ON 1 JANUARY 2026 and must be configured: the relapse period was extended from 14 days to 8 WEEKS, so a new incapacity within 8 weeks of a previous one does NOT start a new guaranteed salary period unless the balance of the previous period is unused or a different illness is proven; and NO guaranteed salary is due for an incapacity arising during a period of progressive work resumption. Sickness days remain equivalent days for holiday accrual. | 250 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes |  |  |

Belgian correction rule for a taxable and contributory item: a correction relating to the CURRENT calendar year is added to the period's ordinary remuneration and taxed once at the ordinary Annex III scale, while a correction relating to a PREVIOUS year is arrears taxed at the exceptional-remuneration percentage keyed on the monthly gross x 13.92. Social security follows the period of work, so the amount is declared through a corrective DmfA for the original quarter at the 13.07% employee rate and the employer rates then in force; this re-opens the special social security contribution band and the social work bonus for that quarter. | 250 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes |  |  |

This is a materially different treatment and requires its own wage type. Taxed at the ordinary Annex III scale in the month of exercise. | 250 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | No | No |  |  |
| 62994 | Free shares / restricted stock units - taxable benefit at delivery | Gratis aandelen / restricted stock units - belastbaar voordeel bij levering |  | Earning | A grant of free shares or the vesting of restricted stock units is not an option and falls outside the Law of 26 March 1999. It is a benefit in kind valued at the ACTUAL VALUE TO THE BENEFICIARY under ITC 92 art. 36 at the moment the shares are delivered, and it is within the NSSO wage concept where the shares are provided by the employer as consideration for work. Taxed at the ordinary scale. Note that a general capital gains tax on financial instruments applies to gains built up from 31 December 2025 and in force from 1 January 2026; it operates OUTSIDE payroll but changes the employee's after-tax analysis on eventual disposal. | 250 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes |  |  |
| BE-001 | Special social security contribution (employee) | Bijzondere bijdrage voor de sociale zekerheid (werknemer) |  | Deduction | Separate EMPLOYEE withholding created in 1994, banded on the QUARTERLY gross wage declared to the NSSO (at 108% for blue-collar workers) and NOT deductible from the taxable base. INDIVIDUALLY ASSESSED EMPLOYEE, 2026 bands per quarter: nil up to EUR 5,836.14; 4.22% x (W - 5,836.14) from EUR 5,836.15 to 6,570.54; EUR 30.99 + 1.10% x (W - 6,570.54) to EUR 11,211.00; EUR 82.05 + 3.38% x (W - 11,211.00) to EUR 12,300.00; EUR 118.83 + 1.10% x (W - 12,300.00) to EUR 18,116.46; maximum EUR 182.82 per quarter above that, i.e. EUR 731.28 per year. JOINT ASSESSMENT WITH A SPOUSE HAVING PROFESSIONAL INCOME: fixed withholding of EUR 15.45 per quarter from a quarterly wage of EUR 3,285.29; the band above EUR 6,570.54 is EUR 43.32 + 1.10% x (W - 6,570.54); maximum EUR 154.92 per quarter. Where the spouse has NO professional income the individual formula applies with the EUR 182.82 maximum. Because the band depends on the QUARTERLY total, the amounts withheld in months 1 and 2 are PROVISIONAL and must be trued up at the last payment of the quarter. Finally settled through the tax assessment. Reported on 281.10 in box 287 (zone 2.075). NOTE: a legislative reform progressively reducing and eventually abolishing this contribution is confirmed to be under way, but the phase-in percentages and the year of conversion to a genuinely individual levy are NOT confirmed against a primary source - do not configure a reduced percentage without that check. | 287 |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | No | No | No | NA | NA |  |  |
| BE-002 | Social work bonus - reduction of the employee contribution | Sociale werkbonus - vermindering van de werknemersbijdrage |  | Deduction | Reduces the employee's 13.07% contribution for low wages and can NEVER exceed the contribution itself, so it cannot create a negative deduction. Since 1 April 2024 the bonus has TWO components, each with its own full-amount limit, maximum and degressive coefficient, computed separately and ADDED: R_B = maximum B if S <= S_B, else maximum B - coefficient B x (S - S_B) while S <= S_A, else 0; R_A = maximum A if S <= S_A, else maximum A - coefficient A x (S - S_A) while S <= S_zero, else 0; social work bonus P = R_B + R_A. S is the reference monthly wage at 100%, full-time equivalent - for a blue-collar worker the wage at 100%, NOT the 108% contribution base. 2026 parameters, selected by the PAY MONTH and not by the year: Component B (bediende/white-collar): full amount up to EUR 2,218.73 (1 Jan and 1 Mar) -> EUR 2,255.50 (1 Apr) -> EUR 2,300.62 (1 Jul and 1 Sep); maximum EUR 165.87 -> EUR 168.62 -> EUR 171.99; coefficient 0.2699 throughout; reaches zero at EUR 2,833.36 -> EUR 2,880.32 -> EUR 2,937.93. Component A (bediende/white-collar): full amount up to EUR 2,833.36 (1 Jan and 1 Mar) -> EUR 2,880.32 (1 Apr) -> EUR 2,937.93 (1 Jul and 1 Sep); maximum EUR 123.00 -> EUR 125.04 -> EUR 127.54; coefficient 0.2807 (1 Jan) -> 0.2442 (1 Mar) -> 0.2738 (1 Apr) -> 0.3196 (1 Jul) -> 0.2739 (1 Sep); reaches zero at EUR 3,271.48 (1 Jan) -> EUR 3,336.98 (1 Mar, unchanged 1 Apr and 1 Jul) -> EUR 3,403.62 (1 Sep). Component B's zero point is always component A's full-amount limit, so the two dovetail. The blue-collar (arbeider) maxima AND coefficients are the white-collar figures x 1.08 (e.g. from 1 Jul 2026 EUR 185.75 / 0.2915 and EUR 137.74 / 0.3452) because the contribution runs on the 108% base; the wage limits are identical for both categories, S being the wage at 100%. CRITICAL - the full-amount limit is the LOWER limit: the maximum applies only at or below it, the band between the two limits is degressive, and above the zero point the component is nil. Granting the full maximum anywhere below the upper limit is wrong and overstates the bonus. PRORATED where the employee does not work a full month or works part-time, using the fraction of full-time performance (H/U or J/D, capped at 1.00), the proration being applied to the full-time-equivalent P. Annual cap EUR 3,466.44 from 1 January 2026, EUR 3,523.92 from 1 April 2026 and EUR 3,594.36 from 1 July 2026. CLOSED 16 September 2026, from the published instructions rather than by extrapolation. NSSO administrative instructions edition 2026/3, the werkbonus annex: 'het totaal van de vermindering per werknemer niet meer bedragen dan 3.594,36 EUR per kalenderjaar'. Cross-checked against the component maxima, which reconcile exactly: EUR 171.99 (component B) + EUR 127.54 (component A) = EUR 299.53 per month white-collar, x 12 = EUR 3,594.36. Applied by each employer on its own reference wage, which for an employee with several employers can produce an over-granted bonus that the NSSO recovers. Legal basis: Law of 20 December 1999, Article 2, implemented by the Royal Decree of 17 January 2000 (BS 28 January 2000), as amended by the Royal Decree of 27 March 2023 (very-low-wage threshold) and the Royal Decree of 5 March 2024 (the two-component split used above); NSSO administrative instructions 2026/1 and 2026/2, the work bonus annex. NOT Article 353bis of the Programme Law of 24 December 2002, which governs the employer-side target-group reduction on restructuring and was cited here in error. | NA |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA |  |  |
| BE-003 | Fiscal work bonus - withholding tax reduction | Fiscale werkbonus - vermindering van de bedrijfsvoorheffing |  | Deduction | Direct reduction of the wage withholding tax for employees ACTUALLY RECEIVING the social work bonus; computed as a percentage of the social work bonus granted in the period and capped on an ANNUAL basis at EUR 765 per calendar year (not indexed) from income year 2025. The reform raises the annual cap to EUR 2,000 from tax year 2027, and the percentage applied to the very low wage component rises from 52.54% to 63% for tax years 2027 and 2028 and to 72% from 2029. No employee declaration is required - it is derived entirely from the social work bonus. Reported on 281.10 in box 284 (zone 2.115) for the work bonus qualifying for the 33.14% tax credit and in box 360 (zone 2.210) for the part qualifying for the 52.54% tax credit. | 284; 360 |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA |  |  |
| BE-004 | Wage withholding tax (bedrijfsvoorheffing) - ordinary remuneration | Bedrijfsvoorheffing - gewone bezoldigingen |  | Tax | PAY-AS-YOU-EARN advance payment of personal income tax, NOT a final tax and NOT cumulative: each payment is taxed as though it were the employee's only income, and the employer carries no year-to-date position and performs NO December true-up. Method for income year 2026: (1) monthly gross; (2) less the 13.07% employee contribution; (3) x 12 to annualise; (4) less lump-sum professional expenses of 30% capped at EUR 6,070; (5) tax on the withholding scale, which is the Annex III nr. 31 base scale with its OWN ceilings, distinct from the Article 130 assessment brackets - 26.75% on 0.01-16,710; 4,469.93 + 42.80% on 16,710-29,500; 9,944.05 + 48.15% on 29,500-51,050; 20,320.38 + 53.50% above 51,050; (6) less 26.75% x EUR 11,170 basic tax-free allowance = EUR 2,987.98, giving the BASISBELASTING; (7) less a SEPARATE flat euro reduction of that basisbelasting for dependent children (Annex III nr. 38, NOT a supplement to the allowance): EUR 624 for one child, 1,656 for two, 4,404 for three, 7,620 for four, 11,100 for five, 14,592 for six, 18,120 for seven, 21,996 for eight, plus 3,864 per child above the eighth, a disabled child counting as two; (8) less the fiscal work bonus, 30% of the personal pension contribution and the overtime relief; (9) floored at zero and divided by 12. The scale percentages are the personal income tax rates of 25/40/45/50% increased by a notional 7% standing in for the regional and municipal surcharges. FROM 1 JANUARY 2026 the taxable base is NO LONGER rounded down to a lower multiple of EUR 15 (Royal Decree of 11 December 2025): the withholding is computed directly and rounded to the eurocent. Scale I where the employee has no spouse or the spouse has own professional income; scale II where the spouse has none; SCALE III for a non-resident who does not earn at least 75% of professional income in Belgium or work at least 75% of contractual time in Belgium - scale III grants NO tax-free allowance and NO family reduction. Where the family situation is not declared, apply scale I with no dependants. Reported on 281.10 in box 286 (zone 2.074; 2.131 for income from the employer and 2.133 from a foreign associated company). | 286 |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA |  |  |
| BE-005 | Wage withholding tax - exceptional remuneration | Bedrijfsvoorheffing - uitzonderlijke vergoedingen |  | Tax | Applies to the end-of-year premium, thirteenth month, bonuses and gratuities, arrears relating to a previous year and the single part of the departure holiday pay. NOTICE INDEMNITIES DO NOT USE THIS TABLE: Annex III nr. 62 routes them to the nr. 58 arrears-and-severance scale (0.00% to 48.00%), and nr. 53 excludes them in terms - see wage type 'Notice indemnity'. Applied to the gross of the payment LESS the 13.07% contribution on that payment. No annualisation and no family reduction. Its low-wage component is calibrated on the same wage levels as the GMMI, so a GMMI increase widens the population that benefits. Applied after the basic and wage moderation contributions are computed. | NA |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA |  |  |
| BE-035 | Regional target-group reduction of employer contributions | Regionale doelgroepvermindering van de werkgeversbijdragen |  | Employer Contribution | Target-group reductions (older workers, first hires in some configurations, specific groups) have been REGIONALISED since the sixth state reform: Flanders, Wallonia, Brussels-Capital and the German-speaking Community each run their own set, and the reduction available depends on the region in which the ESTABLISHMENT UNIT is located. The contribution itself remains federal; only the reduction is regional. This is the principal genuinely geographic variation in Belgian payroll besides the language of the payslip. | NA |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA |  |  |
| BE-036 | Work accident insurance premium - employer | Premie arbeidsongevallenverzekering - werkgever |  | Employer Contribution | Compulsory PRIVATE insurance under the Law of 10 April 1971, risk-rated per employer. An employer cost only; NEVER a payroll deduction and never a benefit to the employee. Note that the 0.30% work accidents branch inside the basic NSSO contribution is a separate item and does not replace this premium. | NA |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA |  |  |
| BE-037 | Sectoral fund contribution - per joint committee | Bijdrage aan het sectoraal fonds - per paritair comité |  | Employer Contribution | Contributions to the funds for existence security and other sectoral funds, set per joint committee and collected through the DmfA. Include for example the closure fund contribution and the 0.10% training contribution due where no sectoral training agreement exists. Depend on headcount and sector and must be configured per joint committee. | NA |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA |  |  |
| BE-038 | Holiday fund contribution - blue-collar (employer) | Bijdrage vakantiefonds - arbeiders (werkgever) |  | Employer Contribution | Employer contribution funding the holiday pay of blue-collar workers, which is paid by the holiday fund under the National Annual Holidays Office rather than by the employer. This is the reason the blue-collar contribution base is the gross at 108% and the employer facial rate is approximately 30.57% rather than approximately 25%. The fund pays 15.38% of the gross of the service year at 108%, of which 8% single and 7.38% double holiday pay. | NA |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA |  |  |
| BE-039 | Non-competition indemnity - employer special levy | Niet-concurrentievergoeding - bijzondere werkgeversheffing |  | Employer Contribution | The non-competition indemnity is not wage for ordinary social security purposes; a SPECIFIC levy applies in its place. Configure alongside 62880 so that the indemnity and its levy are declared consistently in the DmfA. | NA |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA |  |  |
| BE-040 | Decava levy - employer contribution on SWT/RCC supplements | Decava-heffing - werkgeversbijdrage op SWT-aanvullingen |  | Employer Contribution | Specific employer contributions due on the supplementary allowance under the unemployment-with-company-supplement scheme (CBA no. 17 and sectoral CBAs), known as the Decava levies. Rates vary by the age of the beneficiary and by whether the employer is in recognised restructuring. Configure alongside 62882. | NA |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA |  |  |
| BE-041 | Blue-collar contribution base uplift - gross x 108% | Verhoging berekeningsbasis arbeiders - brutoloon x 108% |  | Base | For blue-collar workers the declared wage is multiplied by 1.08 BEFORE contributions are calculated, because their holiday pay is paid by the holiday fund and not by the employer. This applies to the WHOLE contribution calculation, INCLUDING the employee's 13.07% - a frequent configuration error is to apply it only to the employer side. It also feeds the quarterly wage used for the special social security contribution. | NA |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA |  |  |
| BE-042 | Quarterly NSSO wage - contribution base carrier | Kwartaalloon RSZ - drager van de berekeningsbasis |  | Base | The sum of the wages declared for the employee in the DmfA for a CALENDAR QUARTER. Base for BOTH the special social security contribution (BE-001) and the high-wage exemption (BE-033). Because both are computed on the quarterly total, a mid-quarter hire, termination or salary change changes the band: the engine must RECOMPUTE both at the last pay of the quarter rather than treating them as monthly-final. Computed per employer, so a mid-year transfer resets it. | NA |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA |  |  |
| BE-043 | Normal annual gross reference remuneration (monthly gross x 13.92) | Normale jaarlijkse brutobezoldiging (maandbruto x 13,92) |  | Intermediary | Key to the exceptional-payment and double-holiday-pay percentage tables. Computed as the current monthly gross x 13.92, the factor reflecting twelve monthly salaries plus the double holiday pay and the end-of-year premium. This is why a bonus paid to an employee on EUR 3,000 per month is taxed by reference to an annual figure of EUR 41,760 rather than EUR 36,000. For an employee paid other than monthly, the annualised equivalent of the normal periodic pay is used. | NA |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA |  |  |
| BE-044 | Taxable base for the withholding calculation | Belastbare basis voor de berekening van de bedrijfsvoorheffing |  | Base | Gross remuneration LESS the 13.07% employee contribution after the social work bonus, PLUS taxable benefits in kind not subject to social security (company car, free housing, free electricity and heating), LESS statutorily exempt items (bicycle allowance within EUR 0.37/km and EUR 3,700/year, homeworking allowance within EUR 160.99/month, compliant meal and eco vouchers, genuine expenses proper to the employer, CBA no. 90 bonus up to EUR 3,701). The SPECIAL SOCIAL SECURITY CONTRIBUTION IS NOT DEDUCTIBLE from this base, and neither is the employee's personal supplementary pension contribution. | NA |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA |  |  |
| BE-045 | Attachable net income - Article 1409 base | Voor beslag vatbaar nettoloon - basis artikel 1409 |  | Base | The base for the Article 1409 protected bands is the NET income: the gross less social security contributions, the wage withholding tax AND the special social security contribution, computed BEFORE any contractual deduction. Recomputed on the corrected net of the period whenever arrears or corrections change that net. | NA |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA |  |  |
| BE-046 | Overtime hours counter - 180-hour relief ceiling | Urenteller overuren - grens van 180 uren voor de vermindering |  | Intermediary | Counter of the overtime hours actually worked that qualify for the 180-hour relief limit, reported on 281.10 in box 305 (zone 2.142), combining ordinary hours (zone 2.118) and construction hours with a registration system (zone 2.176). Hours are declared IN HUNDREDTHS OF AN HOUR. Drives the point at which relief must stop and 62124 takes over. | 305 |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA |  |  |
| BE-047 | Departure holiday pay offset from the holiday certificate | Verrekening vertrekvakantiegeld op basis van het vakantieattest |  | Deduction | Where a new employee produces a holiday certificate (vakantieattest) from a previous employer, the new employer deducts the corresponding value when the employee takes leave, so that the employee is not paid twice for the same entitlement. The certificate is a MANDATORY take-on document. Where CBA no. 32bis applies (transfer of undertaking) the transferee must instead honour the accrued entitlement directly. | 250 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes |  |  |
| BE-048 | Flag - blue-collar / white-collar status (arbeider / bediende) | Kenmerk - statuut arbeider / bediende |  | Flag | Survives the unified status reform of 2014 for two decisive payroll purposes: holiday pay is paid by the HOLIDAY FUND for blue-collar workers and directly by the employer for white-collar staff, and the contribution base for blue-collar workers is the gross at 108%. Drives the employer facial rate (approximately 25% versus 30.57%), the guaranteed salary scale during sickness, and whether departure holiday pay is settled by the employer at all. | NA |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA |  |  |
| BE-049 | Flag - joint committee (paritair comité) | Kenmerk - paritair comité |  | Flag | Determined by the employer's MAIN ACTIVITY and applicable nationwide within its scope. Drives the sectoral minimum wage scale, the index date and coefficient, the end-of-year premium, sectoral supplements, eco vouchers, sectoral pension and training funds, and sectoral outplacement obligations. Two employers on the same street can owe different premiums because they sit in different joint committees. A change of joint committee opens a NEW OCCUPATION LINE in the DmfA from the effective date. | NA |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA |  |  |
| BE-050 | Flag - DmfA worker code and employer category | Kenmerk - werknemerskengetal en werkgeverscategorie DmfA |  | Flag | Every employee is declared in the DmfA under a WORKER CODE reflecting the applicable scheme (ordinary employee, student with solidarity contribution, flexi-job worker, apprentice) and an EMPLOYER CATEGORY reflecting the sector. These two codes determine which contributions the NSSO actually calculates, so they must be set before any contribution wage type can produce a correct result. Authoritative code lists live in the DmfA code tables and in BE-DD-001. | NA |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA |  |  |
| BE-051 | Flag - withholding scale and family situation (scale I / II / III) | Kenmerk - schaal bedrijfsvoorheffing en gezinstoestand (schaal I / II / III) |  | Flag | Scale I: no spouse, or spouse with own professional income above the threshold. Scale II: spouse without professional income (the marital quotient is anticipated in the withholding). SCALE III: non-resident who neither earns at least 75% of professional income in Belgium nor works at least 75% of contractual time in Belgium - NO tax-free allowance and NO family reduction. Also carries dependants, disability of the employee or a dependant, dependent ascendant aged 65 or over, and single-parent status. Where the employee does not declare the family situation the employer applies SCALE I WITH NO DEPENDANTS, the least favourable lawful position. The employer must not apply an unsubstantiated claim - the risk falls on the employer as withholding agent. | NA |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA |  |  |
| BE-052 | Flag - inbound taxpayer / inbound researcher regime | Kenmerk - regime ingekomen belastingplichtige / ingekomen onderzoeker |  | Flag | Entry conditions: not a Belgian resident and not taxed as a non-resident on Belgian professional income in the 60 months before taking up the post, and not resident within 150 km of the Belgian border in that period. Minimum gross EUR 70,000 per year (nil for researchers). Duration 5 years, extendable by 3. Joint application within 3 MONTHS of taking up the post plus an annual nominative list. THE REGIME DOES NOT CHANGE THE SOCIAL SECURITY POSITION - Belgian contributions remain due unless an A1 certificate says otherwise. 281.10 zone 2.034 flags a foreign executive or researcher under the former regime. | NA |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA |  |  |
| BE-053 | Flag - payslip language regime (employer seat of operation) | Kenmerk - taalregeling loonbrief (exploitatiezetel van de werkgever) |  | Flag | The language of every personnel document is imposed by law according to the EMPLOYER'S SEAT OF OPERATION, not the employee's residence, the contract language or the parties' preference. Dutch-language region: Dutch (Decree of 19 July 1973), sanction NULLITY. French-language region: French (Decree of 30 June 1982), sanction nullity. Brussels-Capital: the language of the EMPLOYEE (Dutch or French), sanction translation at the employer's expense with retroactive effect. German-language region: German. THERE ARE NO BILINGUAL PERSONNEL DOCUMENTS IN BELGIUM, not even in Brussels: each payslip is unilingual. An English payslip is never sufficient on its own and may only be provided as an additional courtesy document. | NA |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA |  |  |
| BE-054 | Flag - A1 certificate / applicable social security legislation | Kenmerk - A1-attest / toepasselijke socialezekerheidswetgeving |  | Flag | Social security follows the PLACE OF WORK, not tax residence, so a non-resident working in Belgium is in the full Belgian scheme. An employee posted to Belgium with an A1 certificate is EXEMPT from Belgian contributions and remains in the home scheme (Regulation (EC) 883/2004 or a bilateral agreement). For an employee working in several Member States the applicable legislation is determined under Article 13 of Regulation 883/2004 with the 25% substantial-activity test. A person may be taxable in one state and insured in another. Posted workers into Belgium also require a Limosa declaration before starting, but it is filed by the foreign posting employer itself - or by the foreign self-employed person under art. 155 - and never by the Belgian payroll client, so the declaration is out of scope for this payroll build (Programme Law (I) of 27 December 2006, arts. 139 and 155; Royal Decree of 20 March 2007); the only Belgian-side duty is the art. 141 check that the declaration was made, an HR onboarding control rather than a payroll step. | NA |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA |  |  |
| BE-055 | Flag - employee proration method (working-day / calendar-day / hourly) | Kenmerk - proratiseringsmethode (werkdagen / kalenderdagen / uren) |  | Flag | Belgium has NO single statutory proration formula; the method is set by the sectoral or company CBA, by the work rules, or failing that by settled practice, and MUST BE APPLIED CONSISTENTLY. Working-day method (market standard for white-collar staff): monthly salary x working days present / working days in the month. Calendar-day method: monthly salary x calendar days of employment / calendar days in the month. Hourly method: monthly salary / 4.3333 / weekly hours x hours worked, where 4.3333 is 52/12. The SAME method must be used for entry and exit so that an employee who joins and leaves in the same year is neither paid twice nor short-paid. | NA |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA |  |  |
| BE-056 | Benefit in kind - free meals provided by the employer | Voordeel van alle aard - kosteloos verstrekte maaltijden |  | Earning | Taxable advantage of any kind under ITC 92 art. 31, 2 degrees, valued under art. 36 at the flat amounts the King has fixed in Article 18, paragraph 3, 6 degrees of the Royal Decree implementing ITC 92 (Royal Decree of 27 August 1993), which Article 18, paragraph 3, 8 degrees makes the valuation basis for meals supplied free of charge: EUR 0.55 per day for the first meal (breakfast), EUR 1.09 per day for the second meal (the main meal) and EUR 0.84 per day for the third meal (the evening meal) - EUR 198.00, EUR 392.40 and EUR 302.40 respectively on an annual basis. Charged per meal per day actually provided. The employee's own contribution is deducted but can never make the benefit negative, the same rule the catalogue applies to employer-provided housing (21400). THESE AMOUNTS ARE NOT INDEXED and have stood unchanged for years, so the 2026 indexation coefficient must not be applied to them. WITHIN THE NSSO WAGE CONCEPT, unlike the company car: the NSSO adopts the identical flat amounts (administrative instructions 2026/2, the wage concept, benefits in kind), so ordinary employee and employer contributions fall due on the same base. A meal voucher must NOT be granted for a day on which a free meal is provided - the two cannot be combined. Reported on 281.10 in zone 2.076 with benefit-nature code E (voeding) in zone 2.099. | 250 |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes |  |  |
| BE-057 | Benefit in kind - PC, tablet, internet connection, mobile phone or telephone subscription for private use | Voordeel van alle aard - pc, tablet, internetaansluiting, mobiele telefoon of telefoonabonnement voor persoonlijk gebruik |  | Earning | Taxable advantage of any kind under ITC 92 art. 31, 2 degrees, valued at the flat ANNUAL amounts fixed by Article 18, paragraph 3, 10 degrees of the Royal Decree implementing ITC 92, as substituted by the Royal Decree of 2 November 2017 (Belgian Official Gazette 13 November 2017, numac 2017013902) with effect from 1 January 2018 and unchanged for 2026: EUR 72.00 per year for a fixed or mobile PC with its standard peripherals and printer, EUR 36.00 per year for a tablet, EUR 36.00 per year for a mobile phone or smartphone, EUR 60.00 per year for an internet connection and its subscription, and EUR 48.00 per year for a fixed or mobile telephone subscription. Charged at one twelfth per month for as long as the device or service is made available. THE AMOUNTS ADD UP PER ITEM, NOT PER EMPLOYEE: a fixed PC plus a home internet connection is EUR 132.00 per year. The internet connection is the single exception - counted ONCE per employee however many devices use it. A benefit arises only from PRIVATE use: where private use is contractually excluded and that exclusion is effectively controlled there is no benefit, and under a split-bill arrangement in which the employee is billed for their own private consumption the subscription benefit does not arise. WITHIN THE NSSO WAGE CONCEPT: the NSSO adopts the identical flat amounts (administrative instructions 2026/2, the wage concept, benefits in kind), so ordinary employee and employer contributions fall due. Reported on 281.10 in zone 2.076 with benefit-nature codes H (PC), I (internet), J (tablet or mobile phone) and K (telephone subscription) in zone 2.099, placed side by side where several apply. | 250 |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes |  |  |
| BE-058 | Benefit in kind - interest-free or low-interest employer loan | Voordeel van alle aard - renteloze lening of lening tegen verminderde rentevoet van de werkgever |  | Earning | Taxable advantage of any kind under ITC 92 art. 31, 2 degrees. Article 18, paragraph 3, 1 degrees of the Royal Decree implementing ITC 92 values it as the difference between a REFERENCE RATE fixed annually by Royal Decree for each loan type and the rate actually charged to the borrower, so a genuinely interest-free loan is valued at the full reference rate. Last published set, income year 2025, by the Royal Decree of 8 February 2026 (Belgian Official Gazette 17 February 2026): mortgage loan at a fixed rate 3.00%; mortgage loan at a variable rate per the reference-index table published in the Gazette; non-mortgage loan with a fixed term a MONTHLY CHARGE percentage of 0.26% for vehicle financing and 0.52% for any other purpose; non-mortgage loan without a determined term, a current account for instance, 5.57%. THE INCOME-YEAR-2026 REFERENCE RATES ARE NOT YET PUBLISHED AND CANNOT BE UNTIL FEBRUARY 2027 - this is a dated future dependency, not an unresolved lookup: on the pattern of every prior year they are set only by the Royal Decree of the following February and are not published until early 2027, so the income-year-2025 rates above are the PROVISIONAL 2026 basis and the benefit must be RECOMPUTED and the 281.10 restated once the definitive rates appear. Do not extrapolate a 2026 rate from the 2024-to-2025 movement. WHAT CLOSES IT: the Royal Decree amending Article 18 of the Royal Decree implementing ITC 92 for income year 2026, expected in the Belgian Official Gazette in February 2027. THE NSSO DOES NOT FOLLOW THESE RATES: for contribution purposes the advantage is valued at its ACTUAL value, the gap against a market-conform rate, and the NSSO treats the loan as a mere wage advance carrying no benefit at all where repayment begins shortly after payment, runs for at most one year, and the amount is reasonable measured against one month of net pay. Reported on 281.10 in zone 2.076 with benefit-nature code A (lening) in zone 2.099. | 250 |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes |  |  |
| BE-059 | Expense reimbursement - costs proper to the employer (justified) | Terugbetaling van kosten eigen aan de werkgever (verantwoord) |  | Earning | Reimbursement of expenses proper to the employer is NOT wage: it is excluded from the NSSO wage concept by Article 19 of the Royal Decree of 28 November 1969, which narrows the concept of Article 2 of the Wage Protection Act of 12 April 1965 as applied for social security purposes by Article 14 of the Law of 27 June 1969, and it is not taxable remuneration under Income Tax Code 1992 Article 31. The exclusion holds ONLY where the reimbursement is genuine and justified, either on actual cost or on a flat rate accepted by the NSSO and the tax administration. PAIR WITH 21149 (Requalification - unjustified flat-rate expense allowance): 21149 requalifies FROM this code, in full, as ordinary wage subject to tax and contributions whenever the expense cannot be justified. On the 281.10 the fiche distinguishes cost-based reimbursements (zone 2.201) and flat allowances meeting serious norms (zone 2.200) from those that do not (zone 2.078), so the justification status drives the zone as well as the requalification. CCG Modules 4.2 and 4.6 require this wage type; before it existed the catalogue carried only the 21149 requalification and had no code for the compliant payment. | NA |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | No | No | No | No | No |  |  |
